20f:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["Reachability follows statement completion, matching the ESLint rule: a case whose every path ends in ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"break"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"continue"}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"return"}],", or ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"throw"}]," (composed through blocks, ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"if/else"}],", loops, labeled statements, and ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"try/catch/finally"}],") needs no ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"break"}],". The groups mirror ESLint’s defaults — arithmetic (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"+ - * / % **"}],"), bitwise (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"& | ^ ~ << >> >>>"}],"), comparison (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"== != === !== > >= < <="}],"), logical (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"&& ||"}],"), and relational (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"in instanceof"}],") — and the rule fires only when both operators fall in the same group."]}]

Arithmetic mixes such as ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"a + b * c"}]," are reported for the same reason. Cross-group pairs (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"a | b && c"}],") and same-precedence pairs (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"a + b - c"}],") are left alone."]}]

34f:["$","p",null,{"className":"x:not-first:mt-[1.25em] x:leading-7","children":["Directive prologues are determined by AST position, not by recognized text: the leading run of string-literal statements at the top of a script, module, namespace body, or function body is accepted whatever it says (",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"\"use strict\""}],", ",["$","code",null,{"className":"nextra-code","dir":"ltr","children":"\"use client\""}],", and any future directive), and the same strings anywhere else are rejected.
